shutil\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import NamedTuple, Optional\n\nfrom .assets import (PACKAGE_ROOT, TOOL_ROOT, asset_candidates, None\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"pi-batch\")\n\n\n# Configuration documents are versioned independently from the package\n# version.  Missing ``config_version`` remains accepted as version 0 so old\n# projects keep working; a future version must fail closed instead of being\n# silently interpreted with today's defaults.\nCONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n\n\ndef _batch_config_candidates() -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Return config candidates in the same order used by the loader.\"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n    script_dir = os.environ.get(\"PBATCH_SCRIPT_DIR\", \"\")\n    if script_dir:\n        candidates.append(Path(script_dir) / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    candidates.append(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    candidates.append(Path(\"pi-batch.yaml\"))\n    candidates.append(Path(TOOL_ROOT) / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    return candidates\n\n\ndef _batch_config_path() -> Optional[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Return the first existing project/tool config path, if any.\"\"\"\n    return next((path for path in _batch_config_candidates() if path.exists()), None)\n\n\ndef _validate_config_version(data: dict, path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject malformed or newer config documents before applying defaults.\"\"\"\n    version = data.get(\"config_version\", 0)\n    if isinstance(version, bool) or not isinstance(version, int):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: config_version must be an integer\")\n    if version < 0:\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: config_version must be >= 0\")\n    if version > CONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"{path}: config_version {version} is newer than supported \"\n            f\"version {CONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION}\")\n\n\ndef _find_batch_config() -> Optional[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Locate and parse pi-batch.yaml: the entry script directory\n    (PBATCH_SCRIPT_DIR, set by the pi-batch.py shim) wins, then the package\n    directory, then the process working directory. None when absent.\"\"\"\n    if not yaml:\n        return None\n    path = _batch_config_path()\n    if path is None:\n        return None\n    data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) or {}\n    if isinstance(data, dict):\n        _validate_config_version(data, path)\n        return data\n    return None\n\ndef _load_batch_config(path: str = \"pi-batch.yaml\") -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Optional defaults for pi-batch. Missing file -> {} (built-in defaults\n    below apply), so the tool still runs standalone with zero config -- copy\n    pi-batch.yaml alongside pi-batch.py to point it at a different agent\n    CLI.\"\"\"\n    data = _find_batch_config()\n    return data if data is not None else {}\n\ndef _find_user_config() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"用户级配置（分层低优先层；项目 pi-batch.yaml 覆盖之）。\"\"\"\n    from . import user_dirs\n    if not yaml:\n        return {}\n    path = user_dirs.user_settings_path()\n    if not path.exists():\n        return {}\n    try:\n        data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) or {}\n    except (OSError, ValueError):\n        log.warning(\"User settings %s unreadable; ignoring\", path)\n        return {}\n    if isinstance(data, dict):\n        _validate_config_version(data, path)\n    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n\ndef _merge_config(project: dict, user: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"项目配置覆盖用户配置（节内键级合并）。\"\"\"\n    merged: dict = {}\n    for section_name in set(project) | set(user):\n        proj = project.get(section_name, {})\n        usr = user.get(section_name, {})\n        if isinstance(proj, dict) and isinstance(usr, dict):\n            merged[section_name] = {**usr, **proj}\n        else:\n            merged[section_name] = proj if section_name in project else usr\n    return merged\n\n_BATCH_CFG = _merge_config(_load_batch_config(), _find_user_config())\n\ndef _section(name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Return a mapping config section, falling back on malformed input.\"\"\"\n    value = _BATCH_CFG.get(name, {})\n    if isinstance(value, dict):\n        return value\n    log.warning(\"Config section '%s' must be a mapping; using defaults\", name)\n    return {}\n\n_AGENT_CFG = _section(\"agent\")\n# Optional per-agent override sections: agent.agents.<bin>.* win over the\n# global agent.* settings for that binary (G line: claude/codex/gemini have\n# different failure signatures and session-flag syntax).\n_AGENTS_CFG = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"agents\")\nif not isinstance(_AGENTS_CFG, dict):\n    _AGENTS_CFG = {}\n\ndef _agent_section(bin_name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Per-agent config section for a binary name (agent.agents.<bin>),\n    or {} when the section is absent/malformed.\"\"\"\n    if not bin_name:\n        return {}\n    section = _AGENTS_CFG.get(bin_name)\n    return section if isinstance(section, dict) else {}\n\n_DEFAULT_ARGUMENT_FLAGS = {\n    \"prompt\": (\"-p\",),\n    \"model\": (\"--model\",),\n    \"provider\": (\"--provider\",),\n    \"thinking\": (\"--thinking\",),\n    \"tools\": (\"--tools\",),\n    \"exclude_tools\": (\"--exclude-tools\",),\n}\n\n\ndef _argument_tokens(value, default: tuple, label: str) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"Normalize one adapter flag without shell-splitting untrusted config.\n\n    An empty string/list disables an optional flag. For ``prompt`` it means\n    the prompt is positional, which is required by CLIs such as Codex.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        return (value,) if value else ()\n    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) and all(\n            isinstance(token, str) and token for token in value):\n        return tuple(value)\n    log.warning(\"Config argument_flags.%s must be a string or string list; using defaults\",\n                label)\n    return default\n\n\ndef agent_extra_args(bin_name: str = \"\") -> tuple[str, ...]:\n    \"\"\"Per-agent trailing args (agent.agents.<bin>.extra_args, falling back\n    to the global agent.extra_args). Used by CLIs that need invocation-level\n    flags outside the six argument_flags slots — e.g. Codex's sandbox mode\n    (``codex exec --approve-for-me``). Empty tuple when absent/malformed.\n    \"\"\"\n    section = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN)\n    value = section.get(\"extra_args\", _AGENT_CFG.get(\"extra_args\"))\n    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) and all(\n            isinstance(token, str) and token for token in value):\n        return tuple(value)\n    if value not in (None, []):\n        log.warning(\n            \"agent extra_args must be a list of non-empty strings; ignored: %r\",\n            value)\n    return ()\n\n\ndef agent_argument_flags(bin_name: str = \"\") -> dict[str, tuple]:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the argv adapter for an agent binary.\n\n    Precedence is agent.agents.<bin>.argument_flags > agent.argument_flags >\n    the historical pi-compatible flag contract. Per-key merging keeps a\n    small override backwards compatible with the existing defaults.\n    \"\"\"\n    global_flags = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"argument_flags\")\n    global_flags = global_flags if isinstance(global_flags, dict) else {}\n    local_flags = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"argument_flags\")\n    local_flags = local_flags if isinstance(local_flags, dict) else {}\n    resolved = {}\n    for key, default in _DEFAULT_ARGUMENT_FLAGS.items():\n        value = local_flags.get(key, global_flags.get(key, default))\n        resolved[key] = _argument_tokens(value, default, key)\n    return resolved\n\n\ndef build_agent_argv(bin_name: str, prompt: str, *, model: str = \"\",\n                     provider: str = \"\", thinking: str = \"\", tools: str = \"\",\n                     exclude_tools: str = \"\",\n                     session_flags: Optional[list] = None) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build an agent argv from the configured adapter, without a shell.\"\"\"\n    flags = agent_argument_flags(bin_name)\n    session_led = bool(session_flags) and any(\n        \"{prompt}\" in token for token in session_flags)\n    if session_led:\n        cmd = [bin_name, *(token.replace(\"{prompt}\", prompt)\n                           for token in session_flags)]\n    else:\n        cmd = [bin_name, *flags[\"prompt\"], prompt]\n    for key, value in ((\"model\", model), (\"provider\", provider),\n                       (\"thinking\", thinking), (\"tools\", tools),\n                       (\"exclude_tools\", exclude_tools)):\n        if not value:\n            continue\n        if not flags[key]:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"agent adapter for {bin_name!r} does not support {key}\")\n        cmd.extend([*flags[key], value])\n    if session_flags and not session_led:\n        cmd.extend(session_flags)\n    cmd.extend(agent_extra_args(bin_name))\n    return cmd\n\n\ndef agent_prompt_index(bin_name: str = \"\") -> int:\n    \"\"\"Index of the prompt value in argv built by :func:`build_agent_argv`.\"\"\"\n    if agent_session_argv_layout(bin_name or AGENT_BIN) == \"led\":\n        for flags in _configured_session_flag_sets(bin_name or AGENT_BIN):\n            for index, token in enumerate(flags, 1):\n                if \"{prompt}\" in token:\n                    return index\n    return 1 + len(agent_argument_flags(bin_name or AGENT_BIN)[\"prompt\"])\n\n\ndef agent_session_argv_layout(bin_name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return whether configured session argv leads or trails the prompt.\"\"\"\n    return (\"led\" if any(\"{prompt}\" in token\n                         for flags in _configured_session_flag_sets(bin_name)\n                         for token in flags) else \"tail\")\n\n\ndef _configured_session_flag_sets(bin_name: str) -> list[list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return valid configured session flag templates for layout inspection.\"\"\"\n    configured = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"session_flags\")\n    if configured is None:\n        configured = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"session_flags\")\n    if not isinstance(configured, dict):\n        return []\n    return [list(flags) for flags in configured.values()\n            if isinstance(flags, (list, tuple)) and flags\n            and all(isinstance(flag, str) for flag in flags)]\n\n\ndef agent_preflight_errors(bin_name: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Return adapter/executable errors before logs, locks or agents start.\"\"\"\n    name = str(bin_name or \"\").strip()\n    if not name:\n        return [\"agent binary is empty\"]\n    errors = []\n    if shutil.which(name) is None:\n        errors.append(f\"agent executable not found or not executable: {name}\")\n    try:\n        agent_argument_flags(name)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        errors.append(f\"invalid adapter for {name}: {exc}\")\n    return errors\n# Default provider/CLI failure signatures (G line). Per-agent overrides:\n#   agent.error_patterns: [...]      (all agents)\n#   agent.agents.<bin>.error_patterns: [...]   (one binary)\n# Empty list disables signature rejection for that agent.\n_DEFAULT_ERROR_PATTERNS = (\n    # provider error codes (quota, rate limit, billing, auth)\n    r\"rate_?limit_?error\",\n    r\"insufficient_?quota\",\n    r\"quota_?exceeded\",\n    r\"credit_?balance_?too_?low\",\n    r\"insufficient\\s+(?:balance|funds)\",\n    r\"billing_?error\",\n# semantics so one noisy or orphan-producing hook cannot wedge a 7x24 run.\nCOMMAND_TIMEOUT = _int_setting(_COMMAND_CFG, \"timeout\", 600, 1)\nCOMMAND_OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _COMMAND_CFG, \"output_max_bytes\", 256 * 1024, 1)\n\n# Progressive memory: preserve pi's raw JSONL sessions, while exposing only\n# a small metadata manifest to the model unless it elects to read more.\nMEMORY_MODE = _choice_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"mode\", \"auto\", (\"auto\", \"on\", \"off\"))\nMEMORY_TENANT = _string_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"tenant\", \"default\", allow_empty=False)\nMEMORY_SENSITIVITY = _choice_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"sensitivity\", \"internal\",\n    (\"public\", \"internal\", \"confidential\", \"secret\", \"regulated\"))\n_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = _MEMORY_CFG.get(\"agent_names\", (\"pi\",))\nif isinstance(_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE, str):\n    _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = (_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE,)\nelif not isinstance(_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE, (list, tuple)):\n    log.warning(\"Config value 'agent_names' must be a string or list; using pi\")\n    _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = (\"pi\",)\nMEMORY_AGENT_NAMES = tuple(str(value) for value in _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE)\n# ── 运行时数据目录（data_dir）───────────────────────────────────────\n# 运行状态（记忆索引/被拒产物/worktrees/设备状态/预算/流程工件/反思）\n# 默认在项目内 .pi-batch/（随仓库共享）；--data-dir / PBATCH_DATA_DIR\n# 可整体移到项目外（如 ~/data/pbatch/<project>），避免污染项目代码结构。\n# 锁保持按 cwd（.pi-batch.lock），与数据位置解耦（单实例语义不变）。\n_DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE = \"\"  # CLI --data-dir（进程级，优先于 env）\n\n\ndef data_dir(cwd: str = \"\") -> Path:\n    \"\"\"运行时数据根：CLI --data-dir > env PBATCH_DATA_DIR > cwd/.pi-batch。\n    env 每次读取（import 后设置也生效）。\"\"\"\n    override = _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE or os.environ.get(\"PBATCH_DATA_DIR\", \"\").strip()\n    if override:\n        return Path(override).expanduser().resolve()\n    base = Path(cwd).resolve() if cwd else Path.cwd()\n    return base / \".pi-batch\"\n\n\ndef set_data_dir_override(path: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"CLI --data-dir 设置（早于 env，进程级）。\"\"\"\n    global _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE\n    _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE = path.strip()\n\n\nMEMORY_INDEX_FILE = _string_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_file\", \".pi-batch/memory/sessions.index.jsonl\", allow_empty=False)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_RECENT = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_recent\", 3, 0)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_SCAN = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_scan\", 30, 1)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_max_bytes\", 4096, 1)\nMEMORY_READ_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"read_max_bytes\", 65536, 1)\nMEMORY_INDEX_LINE_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_line_max_bytes\", 256 * 1024, 1)\nMEMORY_INDEX_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_max_bytes\", 16 * 1024 * 1024, 1)\n\n# F line (round-5 F): provider rate limiting. A process-wide token bucket\n# serializes agent invocations across parallel workers / meta role fan-out;\n# --min-interval only throttles successful SERIAL tasks. 0 = unlimited.\nRATE_LIMIT_PER_SECOND = _float_setting(_LIMITS_CFG, \"per_second\", 0.0, 0.0)\nRATE_LIMIT_BURST = _float_setting(_LIMITS_CFG, \"burst\", 1.0, 0.0)\n_LIMIT_PROVIDERS = _LIMITS_CFG.get(\"providers\")\nif not isinstance(_LIMIT_PROVIDERS, dict):\n    _LIMIT_PROVIDERS = {}\nRATE_LIMIT_PROVIDERS = {\n    str(key): value for key, value in _LIMIT_PROVIDERS.items()\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0\n}\n\n\n# Each fabric capability remains independently off by default. Loading the\n# module never implies scanning, execution, migration, or installation.\n_DEVICE_FABRIC = load_device_fabric(_section(\"device_fabric\"))\nDEVICE_FABRIC_ENABLED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.enabled\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MODE = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mode\nDEVICE_FABRIC_LAN_SCAN = _DEVICE_FABRIC.lan_scan\nDEVICE_FABRIC_BENCHMARK = _DEVICE_FABRIC.benchmark\nDEVICE_FABRIC_AUTO_INSTALL = _DEVICE_FABRIC.auto_install\nDEVICE_FABRIC_APPROVAL_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.approval_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MTLS_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mtls_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_SHORT_LIVED_CREDENTIALS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.short_lived_credentials\nDEVICE_FABRIC_STATIC = _DEVICE_FABRIC.static\nDEVICE_FABRIC_CLUSTERS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.clusters\n\n# Task type classifier (pbatch/classifier.py): deterministic keyword gate\n# that runs BEFORE execution and routes frontend UI tasks to the UI\n# generation pipeline (--classify / `pi-batch.py classify`).\nCLASSIFIER_KEYWORDS = _string_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"keywords\", \"\")\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"frontend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/frontend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\nCLASSIFIER_BACKEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"backend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/backend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\n# Minimum top score for a confident classification (2 = one strong hit).\nCLASSIFIER_MIN_SCORE = _int_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"min_score\", 2, 1)\n# Route when this share of a batch's tasks classify as frontend UI.\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_RATIO = _float_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"frontend_ratio\", 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)\nCLASSIFIER_BACKEND_RATIO = _float_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"backend_ratio\", 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)\nCLASSIFIER_TIE_POLICY = _choice_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"tie_policy\", \"frontend\", (\"plain\", \"frontend\", \"backend\"))\n\n_DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS = {\n    \"start\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\", \"--name\", \"{name}\"],\n    \"continue\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\"],\n    \"fork\": [\"--fork\", \"{session}\"],\n}\n\ndef agent_session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str,\n                        bin_name: str = \"\") -> list:\n    \"\"\"Resolve session flags for a call (start/continue/fork), replacing\n    {session} and {name} placeholders. A {prompt} placeholder is retained\n    for :func:`build_agent_argv` to render at the session-led position. Per-agent override\n    (agent.agents.<bin>.session_flags) wins over agent.session_flags,\n    then pi-style defaults — so a non-pi agent CLI can point these at its\n    own continue-session flags without touching shared config (G line).\"\"\"\n    configured = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"session_flags\")\n    if configured is None:\n        configured = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"session_flags\")\n    cfg = configured if isinstance(configured, dict) else {}\n    flags = cfg.get(key)\n    if (not isinstance(flags, (list, tuple)) or not flags\n            or not all(isinstance(flag, str) for flag in flags)):\n        if flags is not None:\n            log.warning(\"Config session_flags.%s must be a string list; using defaults\", key)\n        flags = _DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS[key]\n    return [f.replace(\"{session}\", session_id).replace(\"{name}\", session_name) for f in flags]\n\ndef _session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Backward-compatible alias resolving flags for the configured bin.\"\"\"\n    return agent_session_flags(key, session_id, session_name)\n\ndef _load_validators() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read validators from the already merged project/user configuration.\n\n    The rest of this module resolves settings from ``_BATCH_CFG``.  Loading\n    the registry from the project file alone would make user-level validator\n    additions and overrides silently ineffective, violating the documented\n    precedence contract.\n    \"\"\"\n    registry = _BATCH_CFG.get(\"validators\")\n    if not isinstance(registry, dict):\n        return {}\n    return expand_validator_registry(\n        registry, PROJECT_FRONTEND_ROOT, PROJECT_BACKEND_ROOT, PROJECT_REPORT_ROOT)\n\nVALIDATORS = _load_validators()\n\nclass ValidatorSpec(NamedTuple):\n    \"\"\"One resolved validator entry.\n\n\"\"\"pi session file access for rotation (R9 / T10).\n\nReads the session files pi writes under ~/.pi/agent/sessions/ (location\nspike-validated in docs/spike-pi-session.md). Used to detect session\ngrowth (rotation watermark) and compaction entries (warn), and to build\nthe --fork invocation that starts a fresh session from the old file.\nEverything degrades gracefully: a missing/unreadable session simply means\n\"no rotation needed\".\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom glob import escape as glob_escape\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom . import config\nfrom .memory_io import bounded_lines\n\n# test-injectable (monkeypatch), default matches pi's runtime layout\nSESSIONS_DIR = Path.home() / \".pi\" / \"agent\" / \"sessions\"\n\n\ndef workdir_key(cwd: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"pi's session-dir key for a workdir: the absolute path with its\n    leading '/' stripped and remaining '/' -> '-', wrapped in '--'. pi\n    writes `--home-u1-project--` for /home/u1/project; dashing the leading\n    slash too produces `---home-u1-project--`, which never matches a real\n    pi session tree (round-4 finding P0-2).\"\"\"\n    path = (cwd or os.getcwd()).lstrip(\"/\")\n    return \"--\" + path.replace(\"/\", \"-\") + \"--\"\n\n\ndef session_file(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Newest session file for an id/name reference under *cwd*.\n\n    Pi normally uses the session id as the filename suffix, while older\n    adapters used the display name; accepting both keeps rotation portable.\n    \"\"\"\n    d = SESSIONS_DIR / workdir_key(cwd)\n    if not d.is_dir():\n        return None\n    try:\n        escaped = glob_escape(session_name)\n        candidates = sorted({path for pattern in (f\"*_{escaped}.jsonl\",\n                                                   f\"*_{escaped}-*.jsonl\")\n                             for path in d.glob(pattern)})\n    except OSError:\n        return None\n    return candidates[-1] if candidates else None\n\n\ndef session_size_bytes(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Size of the active session file (0 when absent).\"\"\"\n    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    if f is None:\n        return 0\n    try:\n        return f.stat().st_size\n    except OSError:\n        return 0\n\n\ndef has_compaction(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the session file contains a compaction entry (pi shrinks\n    the context; worth warning the operator about).\"\"\"\n    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    if f is None:\n        return False\n    try:\n        for line in bounded_lines(f, config.SESSION_LINE_MAX_BYTES):\n            if line is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                if json.loads(line).get(\"type\") == \"compaction\":\n                    return True\n            except ValueError:\n                continue\n    except OSError:\n        return False\n    return False\n\n\ndef fork_flags(session_file_path: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Agent flags that fork the session file into a fresh session (the\n    rotation mechanism; --fork is documented by pi 0.83).\"\"\"\n    from .config import _session_flags\n    return _session_flags(\"fork\", session_file_path, \"\")\n\"\"\"Task execution with hard deadlines, retries, gates, and summaries.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport random\nimport re\nimport signal\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport import clear_marker, is_retryable, task_key, write_marker\nfrom .process_exec import ValidationResult, _DrainTimeout, run_bounded\n# registry of live agent subprocesses (parallel mode: a budget cap must be\n# able to kill in-flight agents so the executor shutdown is prompt)\n_ACTIVE_PROCS: set = set()\n_ACTIVE_LOCK = threading.Lock()\n_PROCESS_POLL_WAIT = threading.Event()\n_BUDGET_MESSAGE = (\"BUDGET: invocation limit reached; stopping — 调整 \"\n                   \"--max-rounds/limits 后可续跑（已保留现场）\")\ndef _scheduled_sleep(seconds: float) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Wait for runner-level retry/throttle scheduling.\n\n    Keeping this boundary separate from the stdlib ``time`` module lets\n    tests replace runner waits without also changing subprocess' timeout\n    polling in other threads.\n    \"\"\"\n    time.sleep(seconds)\ndef _register_proc(proc: subprocess.Popen) -> None:\n    with _ACTIVE_LOCK:\n        _ACTIVE_PROCS.add(proc)\ndef _unregister_proc(proc: subprocess.Popen) -> None:\n    with _ACTIVE_LOCK:\n        _ACTIVE_PROCS.discard(proc)\ndef proc_snapshot() -> tuple[subprocess.Popen, ...]:\n    with _ACTIVE_LOCK:\n        return tuple(_ACTIVE_PROCS)\ndef kill_active_procs() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kill every currently-running agent process group (T8 parallel cap:\n    in-flight agents must not keep the runner waiting after the cap).\"\"\"\n    with _ACTIVE_LOCK:\n        procs = list(_ACTIVE_PROCS)\n    for proc in procs:\n        _kill_group(proc)\ndef _read_stream(stream, prefix: str, collector: list, cap: int = 0,\n                 overflow: Optional[list] = None, emit: bool = False,\n                 target=None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Drain and collect a child stream, optionally displaying it live.\n    Collection is capped at *cap* bytes (T12e): a misbehaving agent must\n    not OOM the runner before the post-hoc size check; overflow is recorded\n    so the caller can reject the result. The stream is still drained (a\n    blocked pipe would hang the agent), while collection and live display\n    stop at the cap.\"\"\"\n    total = 0\n    sink = target or sys.stdout\n    try:\n        for line in iter(lambda: stream.readline(8192), \"\"):\n            within_cap = True\n            if cap > 0:\n                total += len(line.encode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"))\n                within_cap = total <= cap\n            if within_cap:\n                collector.append(line)\n                if emit:\n                    sink.write(f\"{prefix}{line}\" if prefix else line)\n                    sink.flush()\n            elif overflow is not None:\n                overflow[0] = True\n    except ValueError:\n        # stream closed\n        pass\n    finally:\n        stream.close()\n\ndef agent_failure_reason(returncode: int, output: str, bin_name: str = \"\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return a short reason when the agent result must be discarded, or ''\n    when the output is a usable result. Non-zero exit, empty output, and\n    provider/CLI failure signatures (quota, rate limit, auth, billing) all\n    reject the result so error replies are never saved as task outputs.\n    Signatures are per-agent (agent.agents.<bin>.error_patterns can replace\n    the built-in table for claude/codex/... — G line).\"\"\"\n    if returncode != 0:\n        return f\"agent exited {returncode}\"\n    if not output or not output.strip():\n        return \"agent produced no output\"\n    for pattern in config.agent_error_patterns(bin_name):\n        if pattern.search(output):\n            return f\"agent reported provider failure ({pattern.pattern})\"\n    return \"\"\n\ndef _budget_gate(task: Task, key: str, workdir: str, session_name: str = \"\",\n                 trace_task_id: str = \"\", attempt: int = 0) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Stop before spawn when the atomic parallel-safe budget is exhausted.\"\"\"\n    if not budget_try_consume():\n        log.error(_BUDGET_MESSAGE)\n        record_event(\"budget_cap\", task.prompt, \"invocation limit\", workdir,\n                     session_name, task_id=trace_task_id,\n                     stage_id=str(task.memory.get(\"stage\", \"\")), attempt=attempt)\n        clear_marker(key, workdir)\n        raise SystemExit(3)\n\ndef session_file_path(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Active pi session file for a shared/per-stage session (T10).\"\"\"\n    from .session import session_file\n    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    return str(f) if f else None\n\ndef _prompt_brief(task: Task, cmd: list) -> str:\n    \"\"\"One-line log summary with the prompt truncated (log-file safe).\"\"\"\n    brief = \" \".join(task.prompt.split())[:80]\n    flags = \" \".join(config.agent_argument_flags(config.AGENT_BIN)[\"prompt\"])\n    elided = \"...\" if len(brief) == 80 else \"\"\n    return f\"{cmd[0]} {flags} {brief}{elided}\"\n\ndef _kill_group(proc: Optional[subprocess.Popen]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kill the whole child process group, tolerating an already-dead\n    process, then reap the direct child before returning.\"\"\"\n    if proc is None:\n        return\n    try:\n        os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)\n    except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):\n        try:\n            proc.kill()\n        except Exception:\n            pass  # process already gone\n    try:\n        proc.wait(timeout=1)\n    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, ChildProcessError):\n        pass  # 子进程已死/超时：清理路径（已验证有意）\ndef kill_proc_group(proc: subprocess.Popen) -> None:\n    _kill_group(proc)\ndef _spawn_agent(cmd: list, workdir: str, env: dict) -> subprocess.Popen:\n    \"\"\"Spawn the agent in its own process group so the whole child tree\n    can be killed on timeout or interrupt. stdin is /dev/null: prompts\n    arrive via argv (piped stdin prompts are buffered by the CLI first),\n    so an inherited pipe would let stdin-hungry agents (e.g. codex exec\n    appends piped stdin as a <stdin> block) swallow or hang on batch input.\"\"\"\n    return subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,\n                            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True, cwd=workdir,\n                            env=env, start_new_session=True)\n\ndef _agent_not_found(task: Task) -> TaskResult:\n    \"\"\"The agent binary is missing from PATH: a permanent, non-retryable\n    failure (never saved as an artifact).\"\"\"\n    log.error(\n        \"'%s' not found in PATH — 请先安装 agent 或在 pi-batch.yaml 配置 \"\n        \"agent.bin 后重试（本次任务未启动，无副作用）\",\n        config.AGENT_BIN,\n    )\n    return TaskResult(task=task, success=False, stderr=f\"{config.AGENT_BIN} not found in PATH\",\n                      reason=\"agent binary not found\")\n\ndef _context_rejection(task: Task) -> Optional[TaskResult]:\n    try:\n        if not isinstance(task.env, dict) or not all(\n                isinstance(k, str) and isinstance(v, str) for k, v in task.env.items()):\n            raise ValueError(\"task env must map strings to strings\")\n        inject_task_context(task)\n    except ValueError as exc:\n        reason = f\"context injection rejected: {exc}\"\n        log.error(\"REJECTED: %s\", reason)\n        return TaskResult(task=task, success=False, returncode=-1,\n                          stderr=str(exc), reason=reason)\n    return None\n\ndef _task_invocation(task: Task, session_flags: Optional[list],\n                     session_name: str):\n    try:\n        cmd, workdir, session_id, effective_name = _memory_invocation(\n            task, session_flags, session_name)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        reason = f\"agent adapter error: {exc}\"\n        log.error(\"REJECTED: %s\", reason)\n        return TaskResult(task=task, success=False, returncode=-1,\n                          stderr=str(exc), reason=reason)\n    if prompt_limit := _prompt_limit_result(task, cmd):\n        return attach_session(prompt_limit, session_id, effective_name, workdir)\n    return cmd, workdir, session_id, effective_name\n\ndef _finish_result(result: TaskResult, task: Task, session_id: str,\n                   session_name: str, workdir: str, usage_before: Optional[dict],\n                   trace_task_id: str, attempt: int) -> TaskResult:\n    \"\"\"Keep the hot task-execution function within the file budget.\"\"\"\n    return finish_task_result(result, task, session_id, session_name, workdir,\n                              usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n\ndef _stream_task(proc, prefix: str, start: float, timeout: int, emit: bool,\n                 stdout_target=None, stderr_target=None):\n    \"\"\"Forward opt-in display targets without changing legacy mock calls.\"\"\"\n    targets = {}\n    if stdout_target is not None:\n        targets[\"stdout_target\"] = stdout_target\n    if stderr_target is not None:\n        targets[\"stderr_target\"] = stderr_target\n    return _stream_proc(proc, prefix, start, timeout, emit, **targets)\n\ndef run_task(task: Task, task_index: int = 0, total: int = 0, parallel: bool =    trace_task_id = trace_task_id or make_task_id(metering.RUN_ID, task.prompt, task.output, task.workdir(), task_index)\n    stage_id = str(task.memory.get(\"stage\", \"\"))\n    if rejected := _context_rejection(task):\n        annotate_result(rejected, trace_task_id, stage_id, attempt)\n        return rejected\n    invocation = _task_invocation(task, session_flags, session_name)\n    if isinstance(invocation, TaskResult):\n        annotate_result(invocation, trace_task_id, stage_id, attempt)\n        return invocation\n    cmd, workdir, session_id, effective_name = invocation\n    start = time.monotonic()\n    proc = None\n    completed = False\n    prefix, env, key, usage_before = _prepare_task_run(task, cmd, task_index, total, parallel, workdir, effective_name, session_id, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    try:\n        _rate_gate(task, effective_name)\n        proc = _spawn_agent(cmd, workdir, env)\n        _register_proc(proc)\n        write_marker(key, workdir, agent_pid=proc.pid)\n        streamed = _stream_output_enabled()\n        stdout_lines, stderr_lines, overflow = _stream_task(\n            proc, prefix, start, task.timeout, streamed,\n            stdout_target, stderr_target)\n        completed = True\n        if overflow[0]:\n            result = _overflow_result(task, proc, stdout_lines, stderr_lines, start)\n            return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n        result = _result_from_proc(task, proc, stdout_lines, stderr_lines, time.monotonic() - start)\n        result.streamed_output = streamed\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        result = _timeout_result(task, proc, start)\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n        result = _agent_not_found(task)\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except Exception as e:\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n        log.error(\"ERROR  [%.1fs]  [%s]\", elapsed, e)\n        result = TaskResult(task=task, success=False, stderr=str(e), elapsed=elapsed, reason=str(e))\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    finally:\n        if proc is not None and not completed:\n            _kill_group(proc)\n        if proc is not None:\n            _unregister_proc(proc)\n        clear_marker(key, workdir)\n\ndef _prepare_task_run(task: Task, cmd: list, task_index: int, total: int,\n                      parallel: bool, workdir: str, session_name: str,\n                      session_id: str = \"\", trace_task_id: str = \"\",\n                      attempt: int = 0) -> tuple:\n    prefix = f\"[task-{task_index}] \" if (parallel and total > 1) else \"\"\n    log.info(\">>  %s  [model=%s]  [timeout=%ss]  [dir=%s]\",\n             _prompt_brief(task, cmd), task.model or \"default\", task.timeout, workdir)\n    env = {**os.environ, **task.env}\n    key = task_key(task)\n    write_marker(key, workdir)\n    _budget_gate(task, key, workdir, session_name, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    usage_ref = session_id or session_name\n    usage = metering.session_usage(workdir, usage_ref) if metering.EVENTS_FILE else None\n    return prefix, env, key, usage\n\ndef _rate_gate(task: Task, session_name: str = \"\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"F line: wait for a provider token before spawning the agent. Runs\n    AFTER the budget gate so a capped run stops before waiting; blocks\n    parallel workers into a queue instead of overloading the provider.\n    Best-effort: an acquire timeout (or a provider-missing key) only logs.\"\"\"\n    if ratelimit.RATE_PER_SECOND <= 0 and not ratelimit.PROVIDER_RATES:\n        return\n    if not ratelimit.acquire(task.provider or \"\", timeout=300.0):\n        log.warning(\"RATE: token wait timed out for provider '%s' (300s); spawning anyway\",\n                    task.provider or \"default\")\n\ndef _memory_invocation(task: Task, session_flags: Optional[list],\n                       session_name: str) -> tuple[list, str, str, str]:\n    workdir = task.workdir()\n    flags = session_flags\n    name = session_name or task.memory.get(\"stage\", \"\") or \"batch\"\n    session_id = session_id_from_flags(flags)\n    prompt = task.prompt\n    if memory_enabled():\n        prompt = enrich_prompt(prompt, workdir, str(task.memory.get(\"memory_mode\", \"\")))\n        if flags is None:\n            session_id = new_session_id(name, task.prompt, workdir)\n            flags = _session_flags(\"start\", session_id, name)\n    return replace(task, prompt=prompt).to_cmd(flags), workdir, session_id, name\n\ndef _output_path_is_symlink(task: Task) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"T0.1: probe the UNRESOLVED output path — output_path() resolves\n    symlinks away, which would hide a link planted at the output location\n    and redirect the write to its target (round-4 finding H1).\"\"\"\n    probe = Path(task.output)\n    if not probe.is_absolute():\n        probe = Path(task.workdir()) / probe\n    return probe.is_symlink()\n\ndef save_result(task: Task, result: TaskResult) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write a successful task result to its output file, or print to stdout.\n    Failed or rejected results are never written to disk: quota or rate-limit\n    replies must not become committed artifacts, so the error is logged only.\n    \"\"\"\n    out_path = task.output_path()\n    if out_path is None:\n        if not result.streamed_output:\n            sys.stdout.write(result.stdout)\n            if result.stderr:\n                sys.stderr.write(result.stderr)\n        return\n\n    if not result.success:\n        log.error(\"NOT SAVED %s: task failed (exit=%d, %.1fs)\", out_path, result.returncode, result.elapsed)\n        return\n    # T12e: oversized outputs are rejected, not written (runaway agents\n    # must not fill the disk).\n    if len(result.stdout.encode(\"utf-8\")) > config.OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES:\n        log.error(\"NOT SAVED %s: output exceeds %d bytes (T12e cap)\", out_path, config.OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES)\n        return\n\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    # T0.1 (full-SDLC pipeline): refuse symlink targets (a symlinked output\n    # path could redirect the write elsewhere) and use a unique mkstemp\n    # name, mirroring _save_validated's F14 hardening. Note: probe the\n    # UNRESOLVED path — output_path() resolves symlinks away.\n    if _output_path_is_symlink(task):\n        log.error(\"NOT SAVED %s: output path is a symlink (refusing to follow)\", out_path)\n        return\n    fd, tmp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=out_path.name + \".\", suffix=\".tmp\", dir=str(out_path.parent))\n    os.close(fd)\n    tmp = Path(tmp_name)\n    tmp.write_text(result.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    tmp.rename(out_path)\n    log.info(\"WROTE %s  (%d bytes)\", out_path, len(result.stdout))\n    status = _json_status(v.stdout)\n    if status == \"fail\":\n        log.warning(\"REVALIDATION FAILED (JSON status=fail): %s; %s NOT reusable\", cmd, path)\n        return False\n    if status == \"warn\":\n        log.warning(\"REVALIDATE WARN: %s (artifact reusable with warning)\", cmd)\n    if spec.judge:\n        verdict = judge_verdict(v.stdout)\n        if verdict in (\"FAIL\", \"REJECT\"):\n            log.warning(\"REVALIDATION FAILED (judge verdict=%s): %s; %s NOT reusable\",\n                        verdict, cmd, path)\n            return False\n        if verdict is None:\n            log.warning(\"REVALIDATION FAILED (judge: no verdict): %s; %s NOT reusable\",\n                        cmd, path)\n            return False\n    return True\n\ndef _result_from_proc(task: Task, proc: subprocess.Popen, stdout_lines: list, stderr_lines: list, elapsed: float) -> TaskResult:\n    \"\"\"Build a result; failure signatures override a nominal exit code 0.\"\"\"\n    stdout_text = \"\".join(stdout_lines)\n    stderr_text = \"\".join(stderr_lines)\n    # Exit code first, then body presence, then signatures: operational\n    # signatures apply to stderr; the stdout body only rejects line-anchored\n    # banners + short error replies (see runner_results docstring).\n    if proc.returncode != 0:\n        reason = f\"agent exited {proc.returncode}\"\n    elif not stdout_text.strip():\n        reason = \"agent produced no output\"\n    else:\n        # Empty stderr must not trip agent_failure_reason's own output guard.\n        reason = (agent_failure_reason(0, stderr_text)\n                  if stderr_text.strip() else \"\")\n        reason = reason or _stdout_banner_reason(stdout_text, config.AGENT_BIN)\n    success = proc.returncode == 0 and not reason\n    if reason:\n        # P7（使用反馈）：拒绝行内联 stderr 首行（provider 400/model\n        # unavailable 一眼可见，不必翻到下一行的 FAIL 明细）。\n        first_err = (stderr_text or \"\").strip().splitlines()\n        hint = f\" | {first_err[0][:200]}\" if first_err and first_err[0] else \"\"\n        log.warning(\"agent output REJECTED: %s%s\", reason, hint)\n    if success:\n        log.info(\"OK  done  [%.1fs]  [output=%s]\", elapsed, task.output or \"(stdout)\")\n    else:\n        # F2 (retro finding): failures must carry a stderr tail so root\n        # causes are visible in run logs without reopening agent sessions.\n        tail = \"\\n\".join((stderr_text or \"\").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:400]\n        detail = f\"\\n  stderr: {tail}\" if tail else \"\"\n        log.warning(\"FAIL  [code=%d]  [%.1fs]%s\", proc.returncode, elapsed, detail)\n    return TaskResult(\n        task=task, success=success, stdout=stdout_text, stderr=stderr_text,\n        elapsed=elapsed, returncode=proc.returncode, reason=reason or \"\",\n    )\n\ndef _timeout_result(task: Task, proc: subprocess.Popen, start: float) -> TaskResult:\n    \"\"\"Kill the whole process group (the direct child may have spawned\n    helpers that keep pipes open) and return a timed-out result.\"\"\"\n    _kill_group(proc)\n    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n    log.error(\"TIMEOUT  [%.1fs]  [limit=%ss]\", elapsed, task.timeout)\n    return TaskResult(\n  \"\"\"Whether agent response bodies should be displayed while running.\"\"\"\n    mode = str(config.STREAM_OUTPUT).lower()\n    return mode == \"full\" or (mode == \"auto\" and stderr_target=None) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], list]:\n    \"\"\"Drain both pipes to one deadline; report stdout-cap overflow.\"\"\"\n    stdout_lines, stderr_lines, overflow, _ = _drain_process(\n        proc, prefix, start, timeout, config.OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES, emit,\n        stdout_target, stderr_target)\n    return stdout_lines, stderr_lines, overflow\n\n\ndef _drain_process(proc: subprocess.Popen, prefix: str, start: float,\n                   timeout: float, cap: int, emit: bool = False,\n                   stdout_target=None, stderr_target=None) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"Drain both pipes with one absolute deadline and bounded collectors.\"\"\"\n    from threading import Thread\n    stdout_lines: list[str] = []\n    stderr_lines: list[str] = []\n    stdout_overflow: list = [False]\n    stderr_overflow: list = [False]\n    stdout_sink = sys.stdout if stdout_target is None else stdout_target\n    stderr_sink = sys.stderr if stderr_target is None else stderr_target\n    args = (cap, stdout_overflow, emit, stdout_sink)\n    tout = Thread(target=_read_stream, args=(proc.stdout, prefix, stdout_lines, *args), daemon=True)\n    err_args = (cap, stderr_overflow, emit, stderr_sink)\n    terr = Thread(target=_read_stream, args=(proc.stderr, prefix, stderr_lines, *err_args), daemon=True)\n    tout.start()\n    terr.start()\n    deadline = start + timeout\n    tout.join(timeout=max(0, deadline - time.monotonic()))\n    terr.join(timeout=max(0, deadline - time.monotonic()))\n    while proc.poll() is None:\n        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            raise _DrainTimeout(proc.args, timeout, stdout_lines, stderr_lines,\n                                (stdout_overflow[0], stderr_overflow[0]),\n                                (tout, terr))\n        _PROCESS_POLL_WAIT.wait(min(0.05, remaining))\n    return stdout_lines, stderr_lines, stdout_overflow, (compaction entries are warned about). validate_cmd: str = \"\") -> list[TaskResult]:\n    \"\"\"Run serially with validation, retries, throttling, and stable sessions.\"\"\"\n    results = []\n    total = len(tasks)\n    session_active = make_task_id(metering.RUN_ID, task.prompt, task.output, task.workdir(), task_index)\n    stage_id = str(task.memory.get(\"stage\", \"\"))\n    if rejected := _context_rejection(task):\n        annotate_result(rejected, trace_task_id, stage_id, attempt)\n        return rejected\n    invocation = _task_invocation(task, session_flags, session_name)\n    if isinstance(invocation, TaskResult):\n        annotate_result(invocation, trace_task_id, stage_id, attempt)\n        return invocation\n    cmd, workdir, session_id, effective_name = invocation\n    start = time.monotonic()\n    proc = None\n    completed = False\n    prefix, env, key, usage_before = _prepare_task_run(task, cmd, task_index, total, parallel, workdir, effective_name, session_id, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    try:\n        _rate_gate(task, effective_name)\n        proc = _spawn_agent(cmd, workdir, env)\n        _register_proc(proc)\n        write_marker(key, workdir, agent_pid=proc.pid)\n        streamed = _stream_output_enabled()\n        stdout_lines, stderr_lines, overflow = _stream_task(\n            proc, prefix, start, task.timeout, streamed,\n            stdout_target, stderr_target)\n        completed = True\n        if overflow[0]:\n            result = _overflow_result(task, proc, stdout_lines, stderr_lines, start)\n            return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n        result = _result_from_proc(task, proc, stdout_lines, stderr_lines, time.monotonic() - start)\n        result.streamed_output = streamed\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        result = _timeout_result(task, proc, start)\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n        result = _agent_not_found(task)\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    except Exception as e:\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n        log.error(\"ERROR  [%.1fs]  [%s]\", elapsed, e)\n        result = TaskResult(task=task, success=False, stderr=str(e), elapsed=elapsed, reason=str(e))\n        return _finish_result(result, task, session_id, effective_name, workdir, usage_before, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    finally:\n        if proc is not None and not completed:\n            _kill_group(proc)\n        if proc is not None:\n            _unregister_proc(proc)\n        clear_marker(key, workdir)\n\ndef _prepare_task_run(task: Task, cmd: list, task_index: int, total: int,\n                      parallel: bool, workdir: str, session_name: str,\n                      session_id: str = \"\", trace_task_id: str = \"\",\n                      attempt: int = 0) -> tuple:\n    prefix = f\"[task-{task_index}] \" if (parallel and total > 1) else \"\"\n    log.info(\">>  %s  [model=%s]  [timeout=%ss]  [dir=%s]\",\n             _prompt_brief(task, cmd), task.model or \"default\", task.timeout, workdir)\n    env = {**os.environ, **task.env}\n    key = task_key(task)\n    write_marker(key, workdir)\n    _budget_gate(task, key, workdir, session_name, trace_task_id, attempt)\n    usage_ref = session_id or session_name\n    usage = metering.session_usage(workdir, usage_ref) if metering.EVENTS_FILE else None\n    return prefix, env, key, usage\n\ndef _rate_gate(task: Task, session_name: str = \"\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"F line: wait for a provider token before spawning the agent. Runs\n    AFTER the budget gate so a capped run stops before waiting; blocks\n    parallel workers into a queue instead of overloading the provider.\n    Best-effort: an acquire timeout (or a provider-missing key) only logs.\"\"\"\n    if ratelimit.RATE_PER_SECOND <= 0 and not ratelimit.PROVIDER_RATES:\n        return\n    if not ratelimit.acquire(task.provider or \"\", timeout=300.0):\n        log.warning(\"RATE: token wait timed out for provider '%s' (300s); spawning anyway\",\n                    task.provider or \"default\")\n\ndef _memory_invocation(task: Task, session_flags: Optional[list],\n                       session_name: str) -> tuple[list, str, str, str]:\n    workdir = task.workdir()\n    flags = session_flags\n    name = session_name or task.memory.get(\"stage\", \"\") or \"batch\"\n    session_id = session_id_from_flags(flags)\n    prompt = task.prompt\n    if memory_enabled():\n        prompt = enrich_prompt(prompt, workdir, str(task.memory.get(\"memory_mode\", \"\")))\n        if flags is None:\n            session_id = new_session_id(name, task.prompt, workdir)\n            flags = _session_flags(\"start\", session_id, name)\n    return replace(task, prompt=prompt).to_cmd(flags), workdir, session_id, name\n\ndef _output_path_is_symlink(task: Task) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"T0.1: probe the UNRESOLVED output path — output_path() resolves\n    symlinks away, which would hide a link planted at the output location\n    and redirect the write to its target (round-4 finding H1).\"\"\"\n    probe = Path(task.output)\n    if not probe.is_absolute():\n        probe = Path(task.workdir()) / probe\n    return probe.is_symlink()\n\ndef save_result(task: Task, result: TaskResult) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write a successful task result to its output file, or print to stdout.\n    Failed or rejected results are never written to disk: quota or rate-limit\n    replies must not become committed artifacts, so the error is logged only.\n    \"\"\"\n    out_path = task.output_path()\n    if out_path is None:\n        if not result.streamed_output:\n            sys.stdout.write(result.stdout)\n            if result.stderr:\n                sys.stderr.write(result.stderr)\n\"\"\"pi session file access for rotation (R9 / T10).\n\nReads the session files pi writes under ~/.pi/agent/sessions/ (location\nspike-validated in docs/spike-pi-session.md). Used to detect session\ngrowth (rotation watermark) and compaction entries (warn), and to build\nthe --fork invocation that starts a fresh session from the old file.\nEverything degrades gracefully: a missing/unreadable session simply means\n\"no rotation needed\".\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom glob import escape as glob_escape\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom . import config\nfrom .memory_io import bounded_lines\n\n# test-injectable (monkeypatch), default matches pi's runtime layout\nSESSIONS_DIR = Path.home() / \".pi\" / \"agent\" / \"sessions\"\n\n\ndef workdir_key(cwd: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"pi's session-dir key for a workdir: the absolute path with its\n    leading '/' stripped and remaining '/' -> '-', wrapped in '--'. pi\n    writes `--home-u1-project--` for /home/u1/project; dashing the leading\n    slash too produces `---home-u1-project--`, which never matches a real\n    pi session tree (round-4 finding P0-2).\"\"\"\n    path = (cwd or os.getcwd()).lstrip(\"/\")\n    return \"--\" + path.replace(\"/\", \"-\") + \"--\"\n\n\ndef session_file(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Newest session file for an id/name reference under *cwd*.\n\n    Pi normally uses the session id as the filename suffix, while older\n    adapters used the display name; accepting both keeps rotation portable.\n    \"\"\"\n    d = SESSIONS_DIR / workdir_key(cwd)\n    if not d.is_dir():\n        return None\n    try:\n        escaped = glob_escape(session_name)\n        candidates = sorted({path for pattern in (f\"*_{escaped}.jsonl\",\n                                                   f\"*_{escaped}-*.jsonl\")\n                             for path in d.glob(pattern)})\n    except OSError:\n        return None\n    return candidates[-1] if candidates else None\n\n\ndef session_size_bytes(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Size of the active session file (0 when absent).\"\"\"\n    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    if f is None:\n        return 0\n    try:\n        return f.stat().st_size\n    except OSError:\n        return 0\n\n\ndef has_compaction(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the session file contains a compaction entry (pi shrinks\n    the context; worth warning the operator about).\"\"\"\n    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    if f is None:\n        return False\n    try:\n        for line in bounded_lines(f, config.SESSION_LINE_MAX_BYTES):\n            if line is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                if json.loads(line).get(\"type\") == \"compaction\":\n                    return True\n            except ValueError:\n                continue\n    except OSError:\n        return False\n    return False\n\n\ndef fork_flags(session_file_path: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Agent flags that fork the session file into a fresh session (the\n    rotation mechanism; --fork is documented by pi 0.83).\"\"\"\n    from .config import _session_flags\n    return _session_flags(\"fork\", session_file_path, \"\")\n# semantics so one noisy or orphan-producing hook cannot wedge a 7x24 run.\nCOMMAND_TIMEOUT = _int_setting(_COMMAND_CFG, \"timeout\", 600, 1)\nCOMMAND_OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _COMMAND_CFG, \"output_max_bytes\", 256 * 1024, 1)\n\n# Progressive memory: preserve pi's raw JSONL sessions, while exposing only\n# a small metadata manifest to the model unless it elects to read more.\nMEMORY_MODE = _choice_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"mode\", \"auto\", (\"auto\", \"on\", \"off\"))\nMEMORY_TENANT = _string_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"tenant\", \"default\", allow_empty=False)\nMEMORY_SENSITIVITY = _choice_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"sensitivity\", \"internal\",\n    (\"public\", \"internal\", \"confidential\", \"secret\", \"regulated\"))\n_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = _MEMORY_CFG.get(\"agent_names\", (\"pi\",))\nif isinstance(_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE, str):\n    _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = (_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE,)\nelif not isinstance(_MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE, (list, tuple)):\n    log.warning(\"Config value 'agent_names' must be a string or list; using pi\")\n    _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE = (\"pi\",)\nMEMORY_AGENT_NAMES = tuple(str(value) for value in _MEMORY_AGENT_VALUE)\n# ── 运行时数据目录（data_dir）───────────────────────────────────────\n# 运行状态（记忆索引/被拒产物/worktrees/设备状态/预算/流程工件/反思）\n# 默认在项目内 .pi-batch/（随仓库共享）；--data-dir / PBATCH_DATA_DIR\n# 可整体移到项目外（如 ~/data/pbatch/<project>），避免污染项目代码结构。\n# 锁保持按 cwd（.pi-batch.lock），与数据位置解耦（单实例语义不变）。\n_DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE = \"\"  # CLI --data-dir（进程级，优先于 env）\n\n\ndef data_dir(cwd: str = \"\") -> Path:\n    \"\"\"运行时数据根：CLI --data-dir > env PBATCH_DATA_DIR > cwd/.pi-batch。\n    env 每次读取（import 后设置也生效）。\"\"\"\n    override = _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE or os.environ.get(\"PBATCH_DATA_DIR\", \"\").strip()\n    if override:\n        return Path(override).expanduser().resolve()\n    base = Path(cwd).resolve() if cwd else Path.cwd()\n    return base / \".pi-batch\"\n\n\ndef set_data_dir_override(path: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"CLI --data-dir 设置（早于 env，进程级）。\"\"\"\n    global _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE\n    _DATA_DIR_OVERRIDE = path.strip()\n\n\nMEMORY_INDEX_FILE = _string_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_file\", \".pi-batch/memory/sessions.index.jsonl\", allow_empty=False)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_RECENT = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_recent\", 3, 0)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_SCAN = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_scan\", 30, 1)\nMEMORY_MANIFEST_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"manifest_max_bytes\", 4096, 1)\nMEMORY_READ_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(_MEMORY_CFG, \"read_max_bytes\", 65536, 1)\nMEMORY_INDEX_LINE_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_line_max_bytes\", 256 * 1024, 1)\nMEMORY_INDEX_MAX_BYTES = _int_setting(\n    _MEMORY_CFG, \"index_max_bytes\", 16 * 1024 * 1024, 1)\n\n# F line (round-5 F): provider rate limiting. A process-wide token bucket\n# serializes agent invocations across parallel workers / meta role fan-out;\n# --min-interval only throttles successful SERIAL tasks. 0 = unlimited.\nRATE_LIMIT_PER_SECOND = _float_setting(_LIMITS_CFG, \"per_second\", 0.0, 0.0)\nRATE_LIMIT_BURST = _float_setting(_LIMITS_CFG, \"burst\", 1.0, 0.0)\n_LIMIT_PROVIDERS = _LIMITS_CFG.get(\"providers\")\nif not isinstance(_LIMIT_PROVIDERS, dict):\n    _LIMIT_PROVIDERS = {}\nRATE_LIMIT_PROVIDERS = {\n    str(key): value for key, value in _LIMIT_PROVIDERS.items()\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0\n}\n\n\n# Each fabric capability remains independently off by default. Loading the\n# module never implies scanning, execution, migration, or installation.\n_DEVICE_FABRIC = load_device_fabric(_section(\"device_fabric\"))\nDEVICE_FABRIC_ENABLED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.enabled\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MODE = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mode\nDEVICE_FABRIC_LAN_SCAN = _DEVICE_FABRIC.lan_scan\nDEVICE_FABRIC_BENCHMARK = _DEVICE_FABRIC.benchmark\nDEVICE_FABRIC_AUTO_INSTALL = _DEVICE_FABRIC.auto_install\nDEVICE_FABRIC_APPROVAL_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.approval_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MTLS_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mtls_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_SHORT_LIVED_CREDENTIALS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.short_lived_credentials\nDEVICE_FABRIC_STATIC = _DEVICE_FABRIC.static\nDEVICE_FABRIC_CLUSTERS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.clusters\n\n# Task type classifier (pbatch/classifier.py): deterministic keyword gate\n# that runs BEFORE execution and routes frontend UI tasks to the UI\n# generation pipeline (--classify / `pi-batch.py classify`).\nCLASSIFIER_KEYWORDS = _string_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"keywords\", \"\")\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"frontend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/frontend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\nCLASSIFIER_BACKEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"backend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/backend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\n# Minimum top score for a confident classification (2 = one strong hit).\nCLASSIFIER_MIN_SCORE = _int_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"min_score\", 2, 1)\n# Route when this share of a batch's tasks classify as frontend UI.\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_RATIO = _float_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"frontend_ratio\", 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)\nCLASSIFIER_BACKEND_RATIO = _float_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"backend_ratio\", 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)\nCLASSIFIER_TIE_POLICY = _choice_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"tie_policy\", \"frontend\", (\"plain\", \"frontend\", \"backend\"))\n\n_DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS = {\n    \"start\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\", \"--name\", \"{name}\"],\n    \"continue\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\"],\n    \"fork\": [\"--fork\", \"{session}\"],\n}\n\ndef agent_session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str,\n                        bin_name: str = \"\") -> list:\n    \"\"\"Resolve session flags for a call (start/continue/fork), replacing\n    {session} and {name} placeholders. A {prompt} placeholder is retained\n    for :func:`build_agent_argv` to render at the session-led position. Per-agent override\n    (agent.agents.<bin>.session_flags) wins over agent.session_flags,\n    then pi-style defaults — so a non-pi agent CLI can point these at its\n    own continue-session flags without touching shared config (G line).\"\"\"\n    configured = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"session_flags\")\n    if configured is None:\n        configured = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"session_flags\")\n    cfg = configured if isinstance(configured, dict) else {}\n    flags = cfg.get(key)\n    if (not isinstance(flags, (list, tuple)) or not flags\n            or not all(isinstance(flag, str) for flag in flags)):\n        if flags is not None:\n            log.warning(\"Config session_flags.%s must be a string list; using defaults\", key)\n        flags = _DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS[key]\n    return [f.replace(\"{session}\", session_id).replace(\"{name}\", session_name) for f in flags]\n\ndef _session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Backward-compatible alias resolving flags for the configured bin.\"\"\"\n    return agent_session_flags(key, session_id, session_name)\n\ndef _load_validators() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read validators from the already merged project/user configuration.\n\n    The rest of this module resolves settings from ``_BATCH_CFG``.  Loading\n    the registry from the project file alone would make user-level validator\n    additions and overrides silently ineffective, violating the documented\n    precedence contract.\n    \"\"\"\n    registry = _BATCH_CFG.get(\"validators\")\n    if not isinstance(registry, dict):\n        return {}\n    return expand_validator_registry(\n        registry, PROJECT_FRONTEND_ROOT, PROJECT_BACKEND_ROOT, PROJECT_REPORT_ROOT)\n\n# Declarative defaults for pi-batch.py. Optional — pi-batch.py runs fine\n# without this file, falling back to the values below. Copy pi-batch.py +\n# this file into another project and edit `agent.bin` to point at whatever\n# CLI agent you use (pi, claude, codex, gemini, opencode, ...); no code\n# changes needed.\nconfig_version: 1\n\nagent:\n  # The CLI binary invoked for every task. Must accept:\n  #   <bin> -p PROMPT [--model M] [--provider P] [--thinking T] [--tools T] [--exclude-tools T]\n  bin: pi\n  default_model: \"\"     # empty = the agent's own default\n  default_timeout: 1500  # seconds, per task (aligned with pi httpIdleTimeoutMs)\n  default_workers: 4    # parallel mode default worker count\n  # Agent argv adapter. A string is one argv token; []/\"\" disables an\n  # optional capability. prompt: \"\" makes the prompt positional.\n  argument_flags:\n    prompt: \"-p\"\n    model: \"--model\"\n    provider: \"--provider\"\n    thinking: \"--thinking\"\n    tools: \"--tools\"\n    exclude_tools: \"--exclude-tools\"\n  # Session reuse flags used by --session-mode shared/per-stage.\n  # {session} is the session id, {name} the display name. Other agent CLIs\n  # can point these at their own continue-session flags; omit the key to\n  # fall back to these pi-style flags.\n  session_flags:\n    start: [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\", \"--name\", \"{name}\"]\n    continue: [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\"]\n  # G line: failure signatures that reject agent output (quota/rate-limit/\n  # auth/network/CLI banners). Defaults cover pi/OpenAI-compatible providers;\n  # override globally (below) or per binary (agents.<bin>.*). Empty list\n  # disables signature rejection for that agent.\n  # error_patterns:\n  #   - \"rate.?limit\"\n  #   - \"^(?i)error:\"\n  # Per-agent overrides (win over the global agent.* keys): claude/codex/\n  # gemini have different session-flag syntax and failure signatures.\n  agents:\n    codex:\n      # Codex non-interactive mode is a subcommand: `codex exec <prompt>`.\n      # The prompt flag is positional (empty = positional); \"exec\" is the\n      # leading subcommand token that precedes the positional prompt.\n      argument_flags:\n        prompt: [\"exec\"]\n        model: \"-m\"\n      # Invocation-level flags appended after every constructed argv.\n      # --approve-for-me runs inside the workspace-write sandbox with\n      # automatic approval routing (required for non-interactive 24x7 use;\n      # TTY users keep interactive approval via ~/.codex/config.toml).\n      # --json is the official non-interactive transcript mode: stdout is\n      # structured thread/turn/item JSONL and stderr stays operational\n      # (design §9.3); without it codex echoes the full transcript on\n      # stderr, which false-matches failure signatures when the model\n      # quotes project docs (AGENTS.md §2.2). Phase 1 Structured Exec\n      # Adapter consumes the same JSONL.\n      extra_args: [\"--approve-for-me\", \"--json\"]\n      # Codex session resume/fork (`codex exec resume|fork`) is Phase 1\n      # (TUI protocol design §12); use --session-mode new for now.\n      # Prefer precise signatures: codex's stderr transcript ECHOES the\n      # user prompt, so broad words like bare `rate limit` would self-match\n      # (P0 lesson: keep the anti-misfire discipline from AGENTS.md §2.2).\n      error_patterns:\n        - \"insufficient_quota|quota exceeded\"\n        - \"rate.?limit_?error|rate.?limit exceeded\"\n        - \"codex: \"\n\n# Target-project layout. Relative paths resolve against each task cwd and\n# are exposed to validators as {frontend_root}/{backend_root}/{report_root}.\nproject:\n  frontend_root: src\n  backend_root: src\n  report_root: docs\n\n# F line: provider rate limiting. A process-wide token bucket serializes\n# agent invocations across parallel workers and meta role fan-out;\n# --min-interval only throttles successful SERIAL tasks. 0 = unlimited.\n# CLI flags --rate-limit / --rate-burst override these per run.\nlimits:\n  per_second: 0        # max agent invocations per second (0 = unlimited)\n  burst: 1             # token bucket capacity\n  providers: {}        # per-provider overrides, e.g. {openai: {per_second: 2}}\n  # Campaign isolated worktrees share this state through PBATCH_RATE_LIMIT_FILE.\n  # The campaign command sets it automatically; direct runners may set the env.\n\n\n# Preserve pi's original JSONL messages, but inject only a small metadata\n# manifest. The current LLM decides whether to call `pi-batch.py memory`\n# for more history; other agent CLIs remain unchanged in auto mode.\nmemory:\n  mode: auto\n  agent_names: [pi]\n  # Memory queries are scoped by tenant + project digest + sensitivity.\n  # PBATCH_MEMORY_TENANT / PBATCH_MEMORY_SENSITIVITY override these per worker.\n  tenant: default\n  sensitivity: internal\n  index_file: .pi-batch/memory/sessions.index.jsonl\n  manifest_recent: 3\n  # Execute-mode static precheck searches only this many newest metadata rows\n  # for matching domains; it never scans all history on every prompt.\n  manifest_scan: 30\n  manifest_max_bytes: 4096\n  read_max_bytes: 65536\n  # Reject pathological metadata records; raw Pi messages remain untouched.\n  index_line_max_bytes: 262144\n  # Compact metadata at the append boundary; raw Pi sessions are retained.\n  index_max_bytes: 16777216\n\n# Raw session files remain owned by Pi and are never rewritten. These bounds\n    agent.chmod(0o755)\n    inputs = _inputs_dir(tmp_path)\n    base = [\n        sys.executable, str(PI_BATCH),\n        \"--from-dir\", str(inputs),\n        \"--agent-bin\", str(agent),\n        \"--mode\", \"serial\",\n    ]\n    first = subprocess.run(base, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n    assert first.returncode == 0, first.stderr\n    assert counter.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").count(\"x\") == 2\n    second = subprocess.run(base + [\"--reuse\"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n    assert second.returncode == 0, second.stderr\n    assert \"nothing to run\" in second.stderr  # log line goes to stderr\n    assert counter.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").count(\"x\") == 2  # agent not called again\n\n\ndef _recording_agent(tmp_path, args_log):\n    \"\"\"Agent that appends every argv element (one per line) to args_log.\"\"\"\n    agent = tmp_path / \"record-agent.sh\"\n    agent.write_text(f\"#!/bin/sh\\nprintf '%s\\\\n' \\\"$@\\\" >> {args_log}\\necho OK\\n\")\n    agent.chmod(0o755)\n    return agent\n\n\ndef test_shared_session_flags_sequence(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Shared mode: the first call starts the session (with --name), later\n    calls continue it (session id only).\"\"\"\n    args_log = tmp_path / \"args.log\"\n    agent = _recording_agent(tmp_path, args_log)\n    mod = load_batch()\n    mod.config.AGENT_BIN = str(agent)\n    out = tmp_path / \"o\"\n    tasks = [mod.Task(prompt=\"p1\", output=str(out / \"1.md\")), mod.Task(prompt=\"p2\", output=str(out / \"2.md\"))]\n    results = mod.run_serial(tasks, session_mode=\"shared\", session_id=\"sess-1\", session_name=\"props\")\n    assert all(r.success for r in results)\n    lines = args_log.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n    assert lines == [\"-p\", \"p1\", \"--session-id\", \"sess-1\", \"--name\", \"props\",\n                     \"-p\", \"p2\", \"--session-id\", \"sess-1\"]\n\n\ndef test_new_session_has_no_session_flags(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Default mode: every call is a fresh session (no session flags).\"\"\"\n    args_log = tmp_path / \"args.log\"\n    agent = _recording_agent(tmp_path, args_log)\n    mod = load_batch()\n    mod.config.AGENT_BIN = str(agent)\n    tasks = [mod.Task(prompt=\"p1\", output=str(tmp_path / \"1.md\")), mod.Task(prompt=\"p2\", output=str(tmp_path / \"2.md\"))]\n    mod.run_serial(tasks)\n    lines = args_log.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n    assert \"--session-id\" not in lines\n    assert \"--name\" not in lines\n\n\ndef test_per_stage_session_ids(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"per-stage mode derives one session id per stage.\"\"\"\n    args_log = tmp_path / \"args.log\"\n def __init__(self, stdout=(), stderr=(), returncode=0):\n        self.stdout = list(stdout)\n        self.stderr = list(stderr)\n        self.returncode = returncode\n        self.terminate_called = False\n        self.kill_called = False\n        self._waited = False\n\n    def poll(self):\n        return self.returncode if self._waited else None\n\n    def terminate(self):\n        self.terminate_called = True\n\n    def kill(self):\n        self.kill_called = True\n        self.returncode = -9\n        self._waited = True\n\n    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n        self._waited = True\n        return self.returncode\n\n\nclass _BlockingLines:\n    def __init__(self, released: threading.Event):\n        self._released = released\n\n    def __iter__(self):\n        return self\n\n    def __next__(self):\n        self._released.wait(2)\n        raise StopIteration\n\n\nclass BlockingProc(FakeProc):\n    \"\"\"只有 kill 后 stdout 与 wait 才结束的假进程。\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.released = threading.Event()\n        super().__init__()\n        self.stdout = _BlockingLines(self.released)\n\n    def kill(self):\n        super().kill()\n        self.released.set()\n\n    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n        if not self.released.wait(timeout):\n            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(\"codex\", timeout)\n        return self.returncode\n\n\nclass FakeSpawn:\n    \"\"\"记录 argv/cwd 并返回测试预置进程。\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, process: FakeProc, *later: FakeProc):\n        self.processes = [process, *later]\n        self.calls = []\n\n    def __call__(self, argv, cwd):\n        self.calls.append((tuple(argv), cwd))\n        return self.processes.pop(0)\n\n\ndef _run(lines, *, stderr=(), maxsize=256):\n    process adapter.initialize(InitializeRequest(workspace_id=\"ws_test\"))\n    handle = adapter.start_turn(StartTurnRequest(\n        \"PROMPT\", run_id=\"run_test\", turn_id=\"turn_test\"))\n    assert handle.wait(2)\n    adapter.close()\n    return adapter, spawn, list(adapter.events())\n\n\ndef _sample_lines():\n    return [\n        _line({\"type\": \"thread.started\", \"thread_id\": \"output_tokens\": 8,\n            \"reasoning_output_tokens\": 0}}),\n        _line({\"type\": \"turn.started\"}),\n    ]\n\n\ndef A {prompt} placeholder is retained\n    for :func:`build_agent_argv` to render at the session-led position. Per-agent override\n    (agent.agents.<bin>.session_flags) wins over agent.session_flags,\n    then pi-style defaults — so a non-pi agent CLI can point these at its\n    own continue-session flags without touching shared config (G line).\"\"\"\n    configured = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"session_flags\")\n    if configured is None:\n        configured = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"session_flags\")\n    cfg = configured if isinstance(configured, dict) else {}\n    flags = cfg.get(key)\n    if (not isinstance(flags, (list, tuple)) or not flags\n            or not all(isinstance(flag, str) for flag in flags)):\n        if flags is not None:\n            log.warning(\"Config session_flags.%s must be a string list; using defaults\", key)\n        flags = _DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS[key]\n    return [f.replace(\"{session}\", session_id).replace(\"{name}\", session_name) for f in flags]\n\ndef _session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Backward-compatible alias resolving flags for the configured bin.\"\"\"\n    return agent_session_flags(key, session_id, session_name)\n\ndef _load_validators() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read validators from the already merged project/user configuration.\n\n    The rest of this module resolves settings from ``_BATCH_CFG``.  Loading\n    the registry from the project file alone would make user-level validator\n    additions and overrides silently ineffective, violating the documented\n    precedence contract.\n    \"\"\"\n    registry = _BATCH_CFG.get(\"validators\")\n  # Agent argv adapter. A string is one argv token; []/\"\" disables an\n  # optional capability. prompt: \"\" makes the prompt positional.\n  argument_flags:\n    prompt: \"-p\"\n    model: \"--model\"\n    provider: \"--provider\"\n    thinking: \"--thinking\"\n    tools: \"--tools\"\n    exclude_tools: \"--exclude-tools\"\n  # Session reuse flags used by --session-mode shared/per-stage.\n  # {session} is the session id, {name} the display name. Other agent CLIs\n  # can point these at their own continue-session flags; omit the key to\n  # fall back to these pi-style flags.\n  session_flags:\n    start: [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\", \"--name\", \"{name}\"]\n    continue: [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\"]\n  # G line: failure signatures that reject agent output (quota/rate-limit/\n  # auth/network/CLI banners). Defaults cover pi/OpenAI-compatible providers;\n  # override globally (below) or per binary (agents.<bin>.*). log.error(\"REJECTED: %s\", reason)\n    return TaskResult(task=task, success=False, returncode=-1, reason=reason)\n    def _execute_locked(self, task: Task, turn: int,\n                        flags_kind: str) -> TaskResult:\n        lock_path = None\n        try:\n            lock_path = acquire_lock(self.cwd)\n        except SystemExit as exc:\n            reason = f\"workspace is locked (exit {exc.code or LOCK_EXIT_CODE})\"\n            return TaskResult(task, False, returncode=LOCK_EXIT_CODE,\n                              stderr=reason, reason=reason)\n        try:\n            result = self._invoke_runner(task, turn, flags_kind)\n            self._record_result(result)\n            return result\n        finally:\n            release_lock(lock_path)\n\n    def _invoke_runner(self, task: Task, turn: int,\n                       flags_kind: str) -> TaskResult:\n        flags = config._session_flags(\n            flags_kind, self.session_id, self.session_name)\n        if self._cancel_requested.is_set():\n            return self._cancelled_result(task)\n        return runner.run_task(\n            task, task_index=turn, total=1, session_flags=flags,\n            session_name=self.session_name,\n            stdout_target=self._stdout, stderr_target=self._stderr)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _record_result(result: TaskResult) -> None:\n        try:\n            record_task(result)\n        except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n            config.log.warning(\"TUI memory record skipped: %s\", exc)\n\n    @contextmanager\n    def _runtime_config(self):\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
